<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">It was narrated from Salim, from his father, that: He heard the Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him), when he raised his head in the last rak&#x27;ah of the subh prayer, say: &quot;O Allah, curse so-and-so and so-and-so,&quot; supplicating against some of the hypocrites. Then Allah revealed the words: &quot;Not for you is the decision; whether He turns in mercy to (pardon) them or punishes them; verily, they are the wrongdoers</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1078]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1078" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के उन्होंने नबी करीम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) को फज्र की नमाज़ में आखरी रकत से सर उठा कर कुछ मुनाफ़िक़ों पर लानत भेजते सुना. आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) कह रहे थे: &#x27;अल्लाहुम्माल-&#x27;आन फूलनं व फुलना&#x27; (ए अल्लाह! तू फलां फलां को रुसवा कर). तोह अल्लाह त&#x27;आला ने ये आयत नाजिल फ़रमाई: &#x27;आप के इख़्तियार में कुछ नहीं</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1078]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1078" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن عمر رضی اللہ عنہم سے روایت ہے کہ انہوں نے نبی اکرم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کو جس وقت آپ نے فجر کی نماز میں آخری رکعت سے اپنا سر اٹھایا کچھ منافقوں پر لعنت بھیجتے ہوئے سنا، آپ کہہ رہے تھے: «اللہم العن فلانا وفلانا» اے اللہ! تو فلاں فلاں کو رسوا کر ، تو اللہ تعالیٰ نے یہ آیت نازل فرمائی: «‏ليس لك من الأمر شىء أو يتوب عليهم أو يعذبهم فإنهم ظالمون‏» اے محمد! آپ کے اختیار میں کچھ نہیں، اللہ چاہے تو ان کی توبہ قبول کرے یا عذاب دے، کیونکہ وہ ظالم ہیں ( آل عمران: ۱۲۸ ) ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1078]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1078" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke unhone Nabi Kareem (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ko Fajr ki namaz mein akhri rakat se sar utha kar kuch munafiqon par laanat bhejte suna. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) keh rahe the: &#x27;Allahummal-&#x27;an fulanan wa fulana&#x27; (Aye Allah! Tu falaan falaan ko ruswa kar). Toh Allah Ta&#x27;ala ne ye aayat nazil farmai: &#x27;Aap ke ikhtiyar mein kuch nahi, Allah chahe toh unki tauba qabool kare ya azab de, kyunke woh zalim hain&#x27;.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1078]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1078" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1074

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-nasai%3A1079)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 12</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">أَخْبَرَنَا إِسْحَاقُ بْنُ إِبْرَاهِيمَ، قَالَ أَنْبَأَنَا مُعَاذُ بْنُ هِشَامٍ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنِي أَبِي، عَنْ قَتَادَةَ، عَنْ أَنَسٍ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَنَتَ شَهْرًا يَدْعُو عَلَى حَىٍّ مِنْ أَحْيَاءِ الْعَرَبِ ثُمَّ تَرَكَهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1079]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1079" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a></p>

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 12</strong></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">أَخْبَرَنَا قُتَيْبَةُ، عَنْ خَلَفٍ، - وَهُوَ ابْنُ خَلِيفَةَ - عَنْ أَبِي مَالِكٍ الأَشْجَعِيِّ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، قَالَ صَلَّيْتُ خَلْفَ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَلَمْ يَقْنُتْ وَصَلَّيْتُ خَلْفَ أَبِي بَكْرٍ فَلَمْ يَقْنُتْ وَصَلَّيْتُ خَلْفَ عُمَرَ فَلَمْ يَقْنُتْ وَصَلَّيْتُ خَلْفَ عُثْمَانَ فَلَمْ يَقْنُتْ وَصَلَّيْتُ خَلْفَ عَلِيٍّ فَلَمْ يَقْنُتْ ثُمَّ قَالَ يَا بُنَىَّ إِنَّهَا بِدْعَةٌ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1080" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">This source entry preserves two separately attributed reports in their original order. They remain one provider entry, with their distinct narrator introductions and source references. Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: The source narrator reporting Muhammad&#x27;s explicitly attributed speech or action; narrator framing remains part of the account. The narrator&#x27;s stated observations of the Messenger and later leaders are separate from his own concluding innovation judgment; neither is relabelled as another speaker&#x27;s words. Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abu Malik Al-Ashjai&#x27;s father giving his own conclusion to his son. The narrator&#x27;s stated observations of the Messenger and later leaders are separate from his own concluding innovation judgment; neither is relabelled as another speaker&#x27;s words.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="ltr">It was narrated from Abu Malik Al-Ashja&#x27;i that his father said: &quot;I prayed behind the Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) and he did not say the Qunut, and I prayed behind Abu Bakr and he did not say the Qunut, and I prayed behind Umar and he did not say the Qunut, and I prayed behind Uthman and he did not say the Qunut, and I prayed behind Ali and he did not say the Qunut.&quot;</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 2</p>

<p dir="ltr"> Then he said: &quot;O my son, this is an innovation</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1080]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1080" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">अबू मालिक अशजई (सअद) अपने वालिद (तारिक़ बिन अशयम) से कहते हैं के मैं ने रसूलुल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के पीछे नमाज़ पढ़ी तो आप ने दुआ-ए-क़ूनूत नहीं पढ़ी, अबू बक्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) के पीछे नमाज़ पढ़ी तो उन्होंने भी दुआ-ए-क़ूनूत नहीं पढ़ी, उमर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) के पीछे नमाज़ पढ़ी तो उन्होंने भी दुआ-ए-क़ूनूत नहीं पढ़ी, उस्मान (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) के पीछे नमाज़ पढ़ी तो उन्होंने भी दुआ-ए-क़ूनूत नहीं पढ़ी, और अली (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) के पीछे नमाज़ पढ़ी तो उन्होंने भी दुआ-ए-क़ूनूत नहीं पढ़ी, फिर उन्होंने कहा: मेरे बेटे! यह (यानी: मुदावत/लगातार करना) बिदअत है।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1080" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">ابو مالک اشجعی (سعد) اپنے والد (طارق بن اشیم) سے کہتے ہیں کہ میں نے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے پیچھے نماز پڑھی تو آپ نے دعائے قنوت نہیں پڑھی، ابوبکر رضی اللہ عنہ کے پیچھے نماز پڑھی تو انہوں نے بھی دعائے قنوت نہیں پڑھی، عمر رضی اللہ عنہ کے پیچھے نماز پڑھی تو انہوں نے بھی دعائے قنوت نہیں پڑھی، عثمان رضی اللہ عنہ کے پیچھے نماز پڑھی تو انہوں نے بھی دعائے قنوت نہیں پڑھی، اور علی رضی اللہ عنہ کے پیچھے نماز پڑھی تو انہوں نے بھی دعائے قنوت نہیں پڑھی، پھر انہوں نے کہا: میرے بیٹے! یہ ( یعنی: مداومت ) بدعت ہے ۱؎۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1080" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1080" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Malik Ashja&#x27;i (Sa&#x27;ad) apne walid (Tariq bin Ashyam) se kehte hain ke main ne Rasoolullah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke peechay namaz padhi toh aap ne Dua-e-Qunoot nahi padhi, Abu Bakr (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke peechay namaz padhi toh unhone bhi Dua-e-Qunoot nahi padhi, Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke peechay namaz padhi toh unhone bhi Dua-e-Qunoot nahi padhi, Usman (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke peechay namaz padhi toh unhone bhi Dua-e-Qunoot nahi padhi, aur Ali (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke peechay namaz padhi toh unhone bhi Dua-e-Qunoot nahi padhi, phir unhone kaha: mere betay! yeh (yani: mudawamat/lagatar karna) biddat hai.</p>
